&lt;h1&gt;⚔️ Ringout Arena&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;p&gt;Arène 3D vue du dessus. Frappe avec ton épée pour repousser les autres. Le dernier dans le cercle gagne.&lt;/p&gt;

(()=&gt; {
const $=id=&gt;document.getElementById(id), canvas=$('game');
let scene,camera,renderer,clock,ring,ringRadius=12,playerId='',isHost=false,peer=null,hostConn=null,connections=new Map(),players=new Map(),running=false,started=false,seconds=120,frame=0;

const local={id:'',name:'Toi',x:0,z:0,vx:0,vz:0,hp:100,alive:true,color:colors[0],yaw:0,attack:0,dash:0,stun:0,score:0};
let feedLines=[],remoteTargets=new Map(),lastSend=0,lastTick=0;
function toast(s){$('toast').textContent=s;$('toast').style.display='block';clearTimeout(toast.t);toast.t=setTimeout(()=&gt;$('toast').style.display='none',2800)}
function feed(s){feedLines.unshift(s);feedLines=feedLines.slice(0,4);$('feed').innerHTML=feedLines.map(x=&gt;'&lt;div&gt;⚡ '+x+'&lt;/div&gt;').join('')}
function init3d(){
 scene=new THREE.Scene();scene.background=new THREE.Color(0x070b20);scene.fog=new THREE.Fog(0x070b20,28,65);
 renderer=new THREE.WebGLRenderer({canvas,antialias:true});renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(devicePixelRatio,2));renderer.setSize(innerWidth,innerHeight);renderer.shadowMap.enabled=true;renderer.shadowMap.type=THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
 camera=new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(42,innerWidth/innerHeight,.1,100);camera.position.set(0,23,22);camera.lookAt(0,0,0);
 scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0x9abaff,0x16142e,2));
 const light=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff,2.4);light.position.set(-8,18,9);light.castShadow=true;scene.add(light);
 const floor=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(12,96),new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x292f58,roughness:.82,metalness:.18}));floor.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;floor.receiveShadow=true;scene.add(floor);
 for(let r=2.5;r&lt;12;r+=2.35){let m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.RingGeometry(r,r+.025,96),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x6777c4,transparent:true,opacity:.3,side:THREE.DoubleSide}));m.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;m.position.y=.015;scene.add(m)}
 ring=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(12,.18,8,128),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x48dfff}));ring.rotation.x=Math.PI/2;ring.position.y=.18;scene.add(ring);
 const outer=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(12.5,13.2,.9,96,1,true),new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x141d42,metalness:.7,roughness:.4,side:THREE.DoubleSide}));outer.position.y=-.55;scene.add(outer);
 for(let i=0;i&lt;24;i++){let a=i/24*Math.PI*2;let post=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.18,1.1,.18),new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x304a9b,emissive:0x122a70}));post.position.set(Math.cos(a)*12.4,.25,Math.sin(a)*12.4);scene.add(post)}
 const center=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(1.2,48),new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x424b80,metalness:.5}));center.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;center.position.y=.03;scene.add(center);
}
function makeFighter(p){
 const g=new THREE.Group();const color=new THREE.Color(p.color||colors[0]);
 const mat=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color,roughness:.55,metalness:.12});
 const body=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(.38,.62,5,10),mat);body.position.y=.8;body.castShadow=true;g.add(body);
 const head=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.32,16,12),new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xffd6bc}));head.position.y=1.55;head.castShadow=true;g.add(head);
 const helmet=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.34,16,8,0,Math.PI*2,0,Math.PI*.58),new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color,metalness:.45,roughness:.35}));helmet.position.y=1.63;g.add(helmet);
 const armMat=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xd6e3ff,metalness:.6,roughness:.3});
 const arm=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(.12,.42,4,8),mat);arm.position.set(.42,1.03,0);arm.rotation.z=-.5;g.add(arm);
 const blade=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.09,.08,1.35),new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xeaf6ff,metalness:.9,roughness:.18,emissive:0x234c78}));blade.position.set(.52,1.15,.85);blade.rotation.x=.15;g.add(blade);
 const guard=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.38,.1,.12),new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xffd05c,metalness:.7}));guard.position.set(.52,1.14,.22);g.add(guard);
 const shadow=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.55,20),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x020512,transparent:true,opacity:.35}));shadow.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;shadow.position.y=.02;g.add(shadow);
 g.userData={blade,arm,body};scene.add(g);return g;
}
function ensureVisual(p){if(!p.mesh){p.mesh=makeFighter(p)}}
function setupPlayer(p){ensureVisual(p);p.mesh.position.set(p.x,0,p.z);p.mesh.rotation.y=p.yaw||0}
function drawHud(){
 let all=[...players.values()].filter(p=&gt;p.alive);$('count').textContent=all.length;
 $('players').innerHTML=[...players.values()].sort((a,b)=&gt;(b.score||0)-(a.score||0)).map(p=&gt;`&lt;div class="playerrow"&gt;&lt;i class="dot" style="background:${p.color}"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;&lt;span class="rowname"&gt;${p.name}${p.id===playerId?' (toi)':''}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;${Math.max(0,Math.ceil(p.hp))}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;`).join('');
}
function addPlayer(id,name,color){let p={id,name:name||'Combattant',color:color||colors[players.size%colors.length],x:Math.cos(players.size*1.9)*6,z:Math.sin(players.size*1.9)*6,vx:0,vz:0,hp:100,alive:true,yaw:0,attack:0,dash:0,stun:0,score:0};players.set(id,p);setupPlayer(p);drawHud();return p}
function pack(p){return {id:p.id,name:p.name,color:p.color,x:p.x,z:p.z,vx:p.vx,vz:p.vz,hp:p.hp,alive:p.alive,yaw:p.yaw,attack:p.attack,dash:p.dash,score:p.score}}
function broadcast(type,data){for(const [id,c] of connections){if(c.open)try{c.send({type,data})}catch(e){}}}
function sendHost(type,data){if(hostConn?.open)hostConn.send({type,data})}
function connectHost(code){if(!window.Peer){toast('PeerJS ne s’est pas chargé. Recharge la page.');return}
 $('net').textContent='· connexion…';peer=new Peer(undefined,{debug:0});
 peer.on('open',id=&gt;{playerId=id;hostConn=peer.connect(code,{reliable:true});hostConn.on('open',()=&gt;{hostConn.send({type:'join',data:{id,name:'Joueur '+id.slice(0,4),color:colors[Math.floor(Math.random()*colors.length)]}});$('menu').classList.add('hidden');$('net').textContent='· connecté';running=true;feed('Connecté au salon '+code)});hostConn.on('data',msg=&gt;onHostMessage(msg));hostConn.on('close',()=&gt;{toast('Connexion à l’hôte perdue.');running=false;$('net').textContent='· déconnecté'});});
 peer.on('error',e=&gt;{toast('Connexion impossible : '+e.type);$('net').textContent='· erreur'});
}
function createHost(){
 if(!window.Peer){toast('PeerJS ne s’est pas chargé. Recharge la page.');return}
 isHost=true;peer=new Peer(undefined,{debug:0});
 peer.on('open',id=&gt;{playerId=id;local.id=id;local.name='Hôte';local.color=colors[0];players.clear();Object.assign(local,{x:0,z:0,hp:100,alive:true,score:0});players.set(id,local);setupPlayer(local);$('menu').classList.add('hidden');$('net').textContent='· hôte';running=true;feed('Salon hébergé. Code à partager : '+id);$('round').textContent='SALON HÉBERGÉ';toast('Ton code de salon : '+id);$('code').value=id;});
 peer.on('connection',conn=&gt;{conn.on('open',()=&gt;{});conn.on('data',msg=&gt;onHostData(conn,msg));conn.on('close',()=&gt;{let id=conn.peer;connections.delete(id);let p=players.get(id);if(p){p.alive=false;scene.remove(p.mesh);players.delete(id)}broadcast('left',{id});drawHud();feed('Un joueur a quitté le salon.')});});
 peer.on('error',e=&gt;toast('Erreur réseau : '+e.type));
}
function onHostData(conn,msg){
 if(msg.type==='join'){let d=msg.data;if(players.size&gt;=8){conn.send({type:'full'});return}connections.set(d.id,conn);let p=addPlayer(d.id,d.name,d.color);conn.send({type:'welcome',data:{id:d.id,players:[...players.values()].map(pack),host:playerId}});broadcast('joined',pack(p));feed(d.name+' a rejoint la partie');return}
 if(msg.type==='state'){let p=players.get(conn.peer);if(!p||!p.alive)return;Object.assign(p,{x:msg.data.x,z:msg.data.z,vx:msg.data.vx,vz:msg.data.vz,yaw:msg.data.yaw,attack:msg.data.attack,dash:msg.data.dash});return}
 if(msg.type==='attack'){const a=players.get(conn.peer);if(a)hostAttack(a)}
 if(msg.type==='dash'){const a=players.get(conn.peer);if(a)hostDash(a,msg.data)}
 if(msg.type==='start'){startRound()}
}
function onHostMessage(msg){
 if(msg.type==='welcome'){playerId=msg.data.id;local.id=playerId;players.clear();msg.data.players.forEach(d=&gt;{if(d.id===playerId){Object.assign(local,d);players.set(playerId,local);setupPlayer(local)}else{let p={...d};players.set(p.id,p);setupPlayer(p)}});if(!players.has(playerId)){players.set(playerId,local);setupPlayer(local)}drawHud();$('round').textContent='EN PARTIE';return}
 if(msg.type==='state'){let d=msg.data,p=players.get(d.id);if(!p){p=addPlayer(d.id,d.name,d.color)}Object.assign(p,d);setupPlayer(p);drawHud()}
 if(msg.type==='joined'){let d=msg.data;if(!players.has(d.id))addPlayer(d.id,d.name,d.color)}
 if(msg.type==='left'){let p=players.get(msg.data.id);if(p){scene.remove(p.mesh);players.delete(p.id);drawHud()}}
 if(msg.type==='full')toast('Ce salon est complet (8 joueurs).');
 if(msg.type==='event')feed(msg.data.text);
 if(msg.type==='start')startRound();
}
function hostAttack(a){
 if(!a.alive)return;a.attack=.3;let targets=[...players.values()].filter(p=&gt;p.id!==a.id&amp;&amp;p.alive&amp;&amp;Math.hypot(p.x-a.x,p.z-a.z)&lt;2.45);
 targets.forEach(b=&gt;{let dx=b.x-a.x,dz=b.z-a.z,d=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;b.vx+=dx/d*8;b.vz+=dz/d*8;b.hp-=9;if(b.hp&lt;=0){b.hp=0;b.alive=false;feed(b.name+' est éliminé !');broadcast('event',{text:b.name+' est éliminé !'});if(b.mesh)b.mesh.visible=false;}});
 broadcast('state',pack(a));drawHud();checkWinner();
}
function hostDash(a,data){if(!a.alive)return;a.dash=.25;let dx=data?.dx||Math.sin(a.yaw),dz=data?.dz||Math.cos(a.yaw),l=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;a.vx+=dx/l*10;a.vz+=dz/l*10}
function checkWinner(){let alive=[...players.values()].filter(p=&gt;p.alive);if(alive.length&lt;=1&amp;&amp;alive.length===1){let msg=alive[0].name+' remporte la partie !';feed(msg);broadcast('event',{text:msg});$('round').textContent='VAINQUEUR';running=false}}
function startRound(){for(let p of players.values()){p.hp=100;p.alive=true;p.score=0;p.x=Math.cos([...players.keys()].indexOf(p.id)*1.9)*6;p.z=Math.sin([...players.keys()].indexOf(p.id)*1.9)*6;p.vx=p.vz=0;if(p.mesh)p.mesh.visible=true;setupPlayer(p)}seconds=120;running=true;started=true;$('round').textContent='COMBAT';feed('Combat lancé : poussez vos adversaires hors du cercle !');drawHud();if(isHost)broadcast('start',{})}
function attack(){if(!running||!local.alive)return;if(isHost)hostAttack(local);else {local.attack=.3;sendHost('attack',{})}const v=local.mesh?.userData;if(v){v.blade.rotation.x=-.7;setTimeout(()=&gt;{if(v.blade)v.blade.rotation.x=.15},180)}}
function dash(){if(!running||!local.alive)return;let dx=(keys.d||keys.ArrowRight?1:0)-(keys.q||keys.a||keys.ArrowLeft?1:0),dz=(keys.s||keys.ArrowDown?1:0)-(keys.z||keys.w||keys.ArrowUp?1:0),l=Math.hypot(dx,dz);if(!l){dx=Math.sin(local.yaw);dz=Math.cos(local.yaw);l=1}dx/=l;dz/=l;if(isHost)hostDash(local,{dx,dz});else sendHost('dash',{dx,dz});local.vx+=dx*10;local.vz+=dz*10;local.dash=.25}
function step(dt){
 if(running&amp;&amp;local.alive){
 let dx=(keys.d||keys.ArrowRight?1:0)-(keys.q||keys.a||keys.ArrowLeft?1:0),dz=(keys.s||keys.ArrowDown?1:0)-(keys.z||keys.w||keys.ArrowUp?1:0),l=Math.hypot(dx,dz);if(l){dx/=l;dz/=l;local.yaw=Math.atan2(dx,dz)}
 local.vx+=(dx*6.8-local.vx)*Math.min(1,dt*8);local.vz+=(dz*6.8-local.vz)*Math.min(1,dt*8);local.x+=local.vx*dt;local.z+=local.vz*dt;local.attack=Math.max(0,local.attack-dt);local.dash=Math.max(0,local.dash-dt);
 if(isHost){for(let [id,p] of players){if(id===playerId||!p.alive)continue;p.x+=p.vx*dt;p.z+=p.vz*dt;p.vx*=Math.pow(.12,dt);p.vz*=Math.pow(.12,dt);let d=Math.hypot(p.x,p.z);if(d&gt;ringRadius-.2){p.alive=false;p.hp=0;if(p.mesh)p.mesh.visible=false;feed(p.name+' est tombé hors de l’arène !');broadcast('event',{text:p.name+' est tombé hors de l’arène !'});checkWinner()}}}
 let d=Math.hypot(local.x,local.z);if(d&gt;ringRadius-.2){local.alive=false;local.hp=0;if(local.mesh)local.mesh.visible=false;feed('Tu es tombé hors de l’arène !');running=false;if(isHost){broadcast('event',{text:'L’hôte est tombé hors de l’arène !'});checkWinner()}else sendHost('state',pack(local));toast('Éliminé ! Recharge pour rejouer.')}
 if(isHost){if(performance.now()-lastSend&gt;50){lastSend=performance.now();broadcast('state',pack(local));for(let p of players.values())if(p.id!==playerId)broadcast('state',pack(p))}}else if(performance.now()-lastSend&gt;45){lastSend=performance.now();sendHost('state',pack(local))}
 }
 // smooth camera follows the local fighter
 if(local.mesh){camera.position.x+=(local.x-camera.position.x)*Math.min(1,dt*1.8);camera.position.z+=(local.z+22-camera.position.z)*Math.min(1,dt*1.8);camera.lookAt(local.x,0,local.z)}
 // update visuals, animate sword, and ring glow
 for(let p of players.values()){if(!p.mesh)continue;if(p.alive){p.mesh.position.x+=(p.x-p.mesh.position.x)*Math.min(1,dt*12);p.mesh.position.z+=(p.z-p.mesh.position.z)*Math.min(1,dt*12);p.mesh.rotation.y=p.yaw||0;let v=p.mesh.userData;if(v?.blade){v.blade.rotation.x=p.attack&gt;0?-.8:.15}}}
 ring.material.color.setHex(Math.sin(performance.now()*.003)&gt;0?0x4de6ff:0x9a4dff);
}
function animate(t){requestAnimationFrame(animate);let dt=Math.min((t-(lastTick||t))/1000,.04);lastTick=t;step(dt);renderer.render(scene,camera);if(++frame%20===0)drawHud()}
function initLocal(){playerId='local-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8);local.id=playerId;local.color=colors[0];players.set(playerId,local);setupPlayer(local)}
$('host').onclick=createHost;
$('join').onclick=()=&gt;{let c=$('code').value.trim();if(!c){toast('Entre le code du salon.');return}connectHost(c)};
$('attack').onclick=attack;$('dash').onclick=dash;
addEventListener('keydown',e=&gt;{keys[e.key.toLowerCase()]=true;keys[e.key]=true;if(['ArrowUp','ArrowDown','ArrowLeft','ArrowRight',' '].includes(e.key))e.preventDefault();if(e.repeat)return;if(e.key.toLowerCase()==='j'||e.code==='Space')attack();if(e.key==='Shift')dash()});
addEventListener('keyup',e=&gt;{keys[e.key.toLowerCase()]=false;keys[e.key]=false});
canvas.addEventListener('mousedown',e=&gt;{if(e.button===0)attack();if(e.button===2)dash()});canvas.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=&gt;e.preventDefault());
addEventListener('resize',()=&gt;{if(!renderer)return;renderer.setSize(innerWidth,innerHeight);camera.aspect=innerWidth/innerHeight;camera.updateProjectionMatrix()});
init3d();initLocal();drawHud();requestAnimationFrame(animate);
})();
&lt;/script&gt;
&lt;/body&gt;&lt;/html&gt;</textarea>
